```text
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/theory.tex:15:Qwen2.5-14B supports this: d0-vs-d1 AUC drops to 0.643 (vs.\ ${>}0.99$ at 1--1.5B; \S\ref{sec:gen:crossscale}); however, 7--8B results are mixed ($\mathrm{EDD}_{0.70}$ ranging from 4 to 5), suggesting confounds (QLoRA quantization, architecture) interact with the scale effect (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_scale_full}).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/theory.tex:21:Top-$k$ truncation removes the low-probability tokens that carry depth signal, predicting weaker discriminability; top-$k$ cells yield the lowest accuracy (68.9--75.8\%) across the atlas (\S\ref{sec:atlas:strategy}).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/generalization.tex:10:Four classifiers (RF, XGBoost, ordinal MLP, prototypical) converge within 0.8\,pp under cross-model transfer (Appendix~\ref{app:learned_estimator}), confirming a feature-determined accuracy ceiling.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/generalization.tex:106:At 14B (Qwen2.5-14B), self-reference yields 50.8\% ($2{\times}$ chance, $\mathrm{EDD}_{0.70}{=}0$); cross-reference control (Pythia-1.4B) drops accuracy to 44.1\% ($-$6.8\,pp) but raises $\mathrm{EDD}_{0.70}$ to 2 for nucleus and temperature, revealing that ${\sim}$7\,pp of the accuracy drop is attributable to reference-model mismatch rather than scale (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_scale_full}).
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:59:We compute conditional permutation importance (CPI) \citep{strobl2008_conditional}, which permutes each feature \emph{within strata defined by its correlated partners}, isolating the unique contribution after controlling for multicollinearity.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:67:\texttt{var\_surprisal} (avg rank 1.4, CPI\,=\,0.260) and \texttt{p90\_ppl} (avg rank 1.7, CPI\,=\,0.238) are the top-2 in 8/9 cells, with bootstrap rank CI widths of 0.50 and 0.30---both $\leq 0.5$, indicating near-invariant rankings across resamples.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:406:Self-reference (Pythia-1.4B) yields 78.6\%; switching to a cross-architecture reference degrades accuracy by 4--11\,pp, confirming that reference-generator alignment amplifies depth signal.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:577:Each recursive step uses a \emph{different} model family, eliminating the possibility that depth signal arises from a single model's idiosyncratic behavior.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:634:Despite this variation, the classifier remains effective after clipping, confirming that the depth signal resides in the p90-and-below distributional shape rather than extreme outliers.
```
```text
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:647:OLMo exhibits a \texttt{var\_surprisal} rebound at d4 ($2.49 \to 2.83$, non-monotonic), creating a feature signature absent in GPT-2~XL and Pythia.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:818:CORAL calibration improves ordinal structure (d1--d3 BalAcc 40.4\% vs.\ 33.3\% random) without closing the gap to RF (71.6\%), confirming that the bottleneck is the input signal rather than the calibration strategy.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:826:The marginal improvement is consistent across 8 of 9 atlas cells, indicating that Min-K\%'s token-level log-probability signal provides weak but real complementary information to the passage-level distributional features.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:827:The top-2 features (\texttt{p90\_ppl} + \texttt{var\_surprisal}) retain combined importance of 28.7\%, confirming that perplexity tail statistics remain the dominant depth markers even after augmentation.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:851:The 6 shared perplexity features alone achieve 75.9\% (97\% of the full pipeline's accuracy), confirming that mean-level perplexity distributional displacement is the core depth signal.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:884:Merged improvement also varies by generator family: OLMo cells (observer-dissimilar) gain +5.1\,pp on average, compared to +3.9\,pp for Pythia (observer-similar) and +3.5\,pp for GPT-2~XL, suggesting cross-model divergence features contribute more when observer and generator architectures differ.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:890:Merging all 30 features yields a consistent +3.6--3.7\,pp gain over the self-reference baseline across both tested strategies, confirming that cross-perplexity features retain complementary signal even under the 10$\times$ scale gap, though the gain is attenuated relative to +4.2\,pp at 1--1.5B.
/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/docs/paper/section_appendix.tex:950:Under correct strategy routing, four-class accuracy reaches 72.6\% vs.\ 68.0\% under misrouting ($N{=}103{,}498$ / $76{,}502$), a gap of 4.6\,pp; d0 accuracy remains near-ceiling (${\sim}99.6$\%) regardless of routing, confirming that the d0 signal is strategy-invariant.
```

```
[CONTENT_AUDIT_START]

=== 叙事一致性 (CRITICAL 级) ===

- [critical] [叙事一致性] Intro contribution 3 声称 "25% accumulation preserves EDD_{0.70}=3 despite -13--15pp accuracy attenuation"，Discussion 和 Limitations 也引用此结果，但论文正文和附录中无任何 section 呈现该实验的方法、数据、表格或结果。搜遍全部 .tex 文件，"25% accumulation" 仅以断言形式出现在三处（introduction.tex:33, discussion.tex:6, conclusion.tex:5-6），没有实验设计描述、没有表格、没有 methodology。审稿人无法验证此 claim。必须补写 accumulation 实验 section（含方法、表格、数字来源）或从 contribution list 中删除。

- [critical] [叙事一致性] Abstract finding (3) 将 14B 结果表述为正式发现（"signal attenuates with scale"），但正文 §5.4 标题为 "Preliminary Cross-Scale Observations (14B)" 含 "Preliminary" 限定词。Abstract 和 Intro contribution list 均未使用 "preliminary" 对冲。Claim 强度在 Abstract/Intro 与正文之间不一致——审稿人会注意到 "preliminary observations" 被提升为 formal findings。建议 Abstract/Intro 加 "preliminary" 限定，或正文去掉 "Preliminary"（若作者认为证据已充分）。

- [critical] [叙事一致性] method.tex:36 引用 "\S\ref{sec:gen:crossstrategy}"（即 §5.3 Cross-Strategy Transfer）作为 prompted-generation validation 的依据，但 prompted-generation 实验实际在 Appendix A.29 (§app:prompted_gen)，§5.3 完全不涉及 prompted generation。此交叉引用错误会让审稿人翻到 §5.3 后找不到对应内容。应改为 Appendix~\ref{app:prompted_gen}。

=== 章节结构 ===

- [warning] [章节结构] Discussion and Conclusion (§6, discussion.tex) 仅 3 句话（~55 words）。无正式 conclusion（无 "In this work, we..." 总结句），无 synthesis，无 future directions，无 broader implications。实质性讨论被推到 Appendix A.30 (Extended Discussion)，但主文读者不会看到。对于 EMNLP long paper，3 句话的 Discussion+Conclusion 过于单薄，审稿人可能认为论文结构不完整。建议至少扩展到 1 段 conclusion（总结贡献）+ 1 段 discussion（higher-level 解读），即使需要从其他 section 压缩空间。

- [warning] [章节结构] Intro 第 1 段（introduction.tex:4-7）过于密集——同一段内包含 background、problem statement、significance、AND scope statement（full-replacement vs accumulation 的方法论边界声明）。Scope statement ("In this work, we focus on the 1--1.5B parameter regime under controlled full-replacement chains...") 在叙事从 problem→gap 推进时插入方法论细节，打断读者的理解流。建议拆分：P1 讲 background+problem，scope statement 移到 method overview 段或在 gap 段后独立处理。

=== 贡献-范围对齐 ===

- [warning] [贡献-范围] Intro contribution 3 列出的 5 个 sub-claims（cross-model 66.4%, cross-domain 74.1%, 14B 44.1%, 25% accumulation, cross-strategy 45.9%）中，cross-domain arXiv (74.1%) 仅在 Appendix A.17 (cross-boundary) 呈现，正文无对应 section。25% accumulation 完全无实验呈现（见上 critical）。贡献列表中的多项结果缺乏正文支撑——审稿人会质疑为何 contribution 所列结果找不到对应的实验 section。

=== Claim 强度匹配 ===

- [warning] [Claim强度] Abstract "d1--d3 balanced accuracy of 59--82%" 中 82% 是 81.5% 的向上取整（Table 1 GPT-2 XL temp d1-d3: 81.5%）。虽然差异仅 0.5pp，但向上取整定量 claim 是不良实践，审稿人若核对表格会注意到不一致。建议改为 "59--82%" → "59--82%" 保持，或更精确地写 "59--81%"。

- [info] [Claim强度] Intro: "the convergence of four classifiers within 0.8pp" — 实际数据 RF 66.4%, XGBoost 67.2%, MLP 66.7%, Proto 66.6%，range = 0.8pp。严格准确 ✓

=== 实验严谨性 ===

- [info] [实验严谨性] 主结果 Table 1 有 bootstrap 95% CI ✓; cross-scale 有 CI ✓; seed replication 有 std ✓。统计报告质量高。
- [info] [实验严谨性] Baselines 覆盖近年方法：Binoculars (2024), Fast-DetectGPT (2024), DivEye (2026), Min-K% Prob (2024), CORAL (2020), CLOC (2025) ✓
- [info] [实验严谨性] Ablation 覆盖：feature group, reference model, cross-model, cross-strategy, cross-scale, cross-domain, cross-seed, length, depth extension, mixed-depth corpus, classifier comparison。与 method 组件数对齐 ✓

=== 可复现性披露 ===

- [info] [可复现性] GPU (RTX PRO 6000×4), 训练时间 (~200 GPU·hours), software versions (PyTorch 2.10.0, Transformers 5.8.1 etc.), 超参 (LoRA r=16, α=32 etc.) 均完整披露 ✓
- [info] [可复现性] Limitations ✓, Ethics Statement ✓ 均存在且实质

=== 符号/术语一致性 ===

- [info] [符号] 核心符号 ($d$, $K$, $f_\theta$, $\mathcal{D}_k$, $T$, EDD, $\mathbf{f}(x)$) 全文一致，首次出现时有定义 ✓
- [info] [符号] "depth estimation" shorthand 在 §3.1 定义后一致使用 ✓

=== 章节过渡与逻辑流 ===

- [warning] [逻辑流] Discussion (§6) 仅重复 Results 数字（9.3pp strategy gap, 13-15pp accumulation drop, 14B attenuation），没有 higher-level 解读。仅有的解释（"perplexity tails because recursive training shifts the bulk distribution only gradually"）是一句话。按审计标准，Discussion 不应只重述数字，应提供更高层次的含义、对领域的启示、与理论预测的对比等。实质性讨论（provenance toolkit、curation implications）全在 Appendix A.30。

=== Limitations 诚实度 ===

- [info] [Limitations] 3 段覆盖 3 个 distinct limitations（full-replacement 设定、scale/generalization 边界、reference model 依赖），内容实质性强，不是回避式。符合用户规则"覆盖 3 个点左右" ✓
- [info] [Limitations] 字数约 120-130 words，略低于 150 words 通用标准，但用户规则明确"不需要写得很长"，优先遵守用户规则 ✓

=== 去 AI 风格 ===

- [info] [去AI] em-dash (—) 正文 .tex 全部清零 ✓（仅 drafts/ 草稿中有，不影响提交）
- [info] [去AI] en-dash ( – ) 清零 ✓
- [info] [去AI] filler words (notably, importantly, it is worth noting, furthermore, moreover, additionally) 清零 ✓
- [info] [去AI] 黑名单用词 (delve, leverage, utilize, facilitate, myriad, plethora) 清零 ✓

- [warning] [去AI] 正文中 ", confirming that..." / ", suggesting that..." / ", indicating that..." / ", predicting..." / ", revealing that..." / ", establishing..." 等 -ing 分词短语结尾出现 ~25 次（正文 ~10 次，附录 ~15 次）。密度偏高。代表性位置：atlas.tex:101 "suggesting an architecture-independent depth ceiling"；generalization.tex:10 "confirming a feature-determined accuracy ceiling"；generalization.tex:106 "revealing that ~7pp of the accuracy drop is attributable to..."。虽然每个单独看都是合法的科学英语，但集中出现是 AI 写作特征。建议对正文中的实例逐一改写：部分改为独立句，部分改为 "which confirms" 从句。

=== 用户自定义规则检查 ===

- [info] [写作规则] Limitations 章节覆盖 3 个点，篇幅适中 ✓
- [info] [写作规则] 论文 PDF 中不含 ARR Responsible NLP Checklist（由用户在 OpenReview 填写）✓
- [info] [审稿规则] 使用 \usepackage[review]{acl} 模板，启用行号 ✓
- [info] [审稿规则] Phase 1 报告 26 页（含附录），主文页数需确认 ≤8 页（编译已通过）

[CONTENT_AUDIT_END]
```
